PREDS:

tell me the price of a albumin?
----------
PREDS:
select distinct cost.cost from cost where cost.event_type = 'prescriptions' and cost.event_id in ( select prescriptions.row_id from prescriptions where prescriptions.drug = 'albumin' )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['cost.cost', 'prescriptions.row_id'], 'tab': ['cost', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['cost.event_type', '=', 'prescriptions'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'albumin']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select distinct cost.cost from cost where cost.event_type = 'labevents' and cost.event_id in ( select labevents.row_id from labevents where labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'albumin' ) )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['cost.cost', 'd_labitems.itemid', 'labevents.row_id'], 'tab': ['cost', 'd_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['cost.event_type', '=', 'labevents'], ['d_labitems.label', '=', 'albumin']]}

tell me the percentile of the bands value of 1.0 among patients of the same age as patient 27465 on their first hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
select distinct t1.c1 from ( select chartevents.valuenum, percent_rank() over ( order by chartevents.valuenum ) as c1 from chartevents where chartevents.itemid in ( select d_items.itemid from d_items where d_items.label = 'bands' ) and chartevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.age = ( select admissions.age from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 27465 and admissions.dischtime is not null order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1 ) ) ) as t1 where t1.valuenum = 1.0
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.age', 'admissions.hadm_id', 'chartevents.valuenum', 'd_items.itemid'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'chartevents', 'd_items'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '27465'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'bands'], ['t1.valuenum', '=', '1.0']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select distinct t1.c1 from ( select labevents.valuenum, percent_rank() over ( order by labevents.valuenum ) as c1 from labevents where labevents.itemid in ( select d_labitems.itemid from d_labitems where d_labitems.label = 'bands' ) and labevents.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.age = ( select admissions.age from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 27465 and admissions.dischtime is not null order by admissions.admittime asc limit 1 ) ) ) as t1 where t1.valuenum = 1.0
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.age', 'admissions.hadm_id', 'd_labitems.itemid', 'labevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '27465'], ['d_labitems.label', '=', 'bands'], ['t1.valuenum', '=', '1.0']]}

how many hours have passed since patient 30826 stayed in the ward 12 for the last time during the current hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
select 24 * ( strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',transfers.intime) ) from transfers where transfers.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 30826 and admissions.dischtime is null ) and transfers.wardid = 12 order by transfers.intime desc limit 1
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'transfers.intime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'transfers'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '30826'], ['transfers.wardid', '=', '12']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select 24 * ( strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',transfers.intime) ) from transfers where transfers.icustay_id in ( select icustays.icustay_id from icustays where icustays.hadm_id in ( select admissions.hadm_id from admissions where admissions.subject_id = 30826 and admissions.dischtime is null ) ) and transfers.wardid = 12 order by transfers.intime desc limit 1
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'icustays.icustay_id', 'transfers.intime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'icustays', 'transfers'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '30826'], ['transfers.wardid', '=', '12']]}

----------
PREDS:

----------

what are the three year survival rates for those who were prescribed propofol after having been diagnosed with calculus of ureter?
----------
PREDS:
select sum( case when patients.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%j',patients.dod) - strftime('%j',t4.charttime) > 3 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) from ( select t2.subject_id, t2.charttime from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'calculus of ureter' ) group by admissions.subject_id having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',t1.charttime) > 3 * 365 ) as t2 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'propofol' ) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id where t2.charttime < t3.startdate ) as t4 join patients on t4.subject_id = patients.subject_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'prescriptions.startdate', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.charttime', 't2.subject_id', 't4.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'calculus of ureter'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'propofol'], ["strftime('%j','2105-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',t1.charttime)", '>', '3 * 365'], ['t2.charttime', '<', 't3.startdate']]}
----------
TRUTH:
select sum( case when patients.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%j',patients.dod) - strftime('%j',t4.charttime) > 3 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) from ( select t2.subject_id, t2.charttime from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where diagnoses_icd.icd9_code = ( select d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.short_title = 'calculus of ureter' ) group by admissions.subject_id having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%j',current_time) - strftime('%j',t1.charttime) > 3 * 365 ) as t2 join ( select admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.startdate from prescriptions join admissions on prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where prescriptions.drug = 'propofol' ) as t3 on t2.subject_id = t3.subject_id where t2.charttime < t3.startdate ) as t4 join patients on t4.subject_id = patients.subject_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'prescriptions.startdate', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.charttime', 't2.subject_id', 't4.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.short_title', '=', 'calculus of ureter'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'propofol'], ["strftime('%j','2105-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',t1.charttime)", '>', '3 * 365'], ['t2.charttime', '<', 't3.startdate']]}

how many patients received a pericardiocentesis procedure within the same month after receiving a open liver biopsy procedure during this year?
----------
PREDS:
select count(distinct t1.subject_id) from (select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.short_title = 'open liver biopsy') and strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', current_timestamp)) as t1 join (select admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime from procedures_icd join admissions on procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id where procedures_icd.icd9_code = (select d_icd_procedures.icd9_code from d_icd_procedures where d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'pericardiocentesis') and strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', current_timestamp)) as t2 on t1.subject_id = t2.subject_id where t1.charttime < t2.charttime and datetime(t1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd9_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'pericardiocentesis'], ['d_icd_procedures.short_title', '=', 'open liver biopsy'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2105-12-31 23:59:00'stamp)"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

PRED: select d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code in ( select t4.icd9_code from ( select t3.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by t3.c1 asc ) as c2 from ( select t2.icd9_code, sum( case when patients.dod is null then 0 when strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',t2.charttime) <= 5 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) as c1 from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.icd9_code, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id group by admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%J','2105-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%J',t1.charttime) > 5 * 365 ) as t2 join patients on t2.subject_id = patients.subject_id group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 ) as t4 where t4.c2 <= 3 )
GOLD: select d_icd_diagnoses.short_title from d_icd_diagnoses where d_icd_diagnoses.icd9_code in ( select t4.icd9_code from ( select t3.icd9_code, dense_rank() over ( order by t3.c1 desc ) as c2 from ( select t2.icd9_code, 100 - sum( case when patients.dod is null then 1 when strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',t2.charttime) > 5 * 365 then 1 else 0 end ) * 100 / count(*) as c1 from ( select t1.subject_id, t1.icd9_code, t1.charttime from ( select admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime from diagnoses_icd join admissions on diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id group by admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd9_code having min(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) as t1 where strftime('%J','2105-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%J',t1.charttime) > 5 * 365 ) as t2 join patients on t2.subject_id = patients.subject_id group by t2.icd9_code ) as t3 ) as t4 where t4.c2 <= 3 )
